int*bsearch(int*p, int* q, int x) {
if (p==q) 
return (*p)= = x   ? p:NULL; 
int* r=(A); 
if ((*r)<x) 
return bsearch(r+1, q, x); 
else 
return bsearch (p,r,x); 
}

Procedure g(Var Start: Nodeptr);
Var 
p,q,r: Nodeptr; 
begin 
p:=start; 
q:=Nil; 
while p<> nil do 
begin 
r:=q; q:=p; 
p:=p^.Link; 
q^.Link:=r; 
end; 
Start:=q; 
end;

procedure print (L: listpointer);
begin 
if (L<>nil) then begin 
if tag(L)=0 then 
write (data(L)) 
else 
print (data(L)); 
print (Link(L)); 
end 
end; 
P1:=First(L);
while P1<>end(L) do begin 
P2:=P1; 
while P2<>end(L) do begin 
P2:=Next(P2,L); 
P3:=First(L); 
while P3<>P2 do P3:=Next(P3,L) 
end; 
P1:=Next(P1,L); 
end;

function count(t:tree): integer; 
begin 
if t=nil then count:= o 
else if t^.Lchild=nil and t^.rchild=nil then count:=1 
else 
count:=count (t^.Lchild)+count (t^.rchild); 
end;

function find (x, t: nodeptr): nodeptr; 
var q, r: nodeptr; 
begin 
q=t^.down; 
while q<>nil do 
if q=x then return(t) 
else begin 
r:=find(x,q); 
if r <>nil then return(r) 
else q:=q^.right 
end; 
return (nil) 
end;

ﻫﺎي ﻣﺮﺗﺐ ﻣﺮﺑﻮط ﺑـﻪ ﻣﺒﺤـﺚ روش 
 
ﻫـﺎي ﺣﺮﯾـﺼﺎﻧﻪ از

ﻫﺎﺳﺖ
. 
اﻟﮕﻮرﯾﺘﻢ آن ﺑﻪ ﻗﺮار زﯾﺮ اﺳﺖ
   :
Tree (n) 
{ 
for (i=1; i<=  n-1 ; i++) 
{ 
Pt=new(treenode); 
Pt->Lchild=Least(list); 
Pt-> rchild=Least(list); 
Pt->weight=pt-> Lchild ->weight+pt->rchild->weight; 
Insert(list,  pt); 
}

Procedure dfs(V:Vertex) 
begin 
writeln(data(V)); 
Visited[V]:=true; 
for (each vertex W adjacent to V) do 
if (not visited[W]) then dfs(W); 
end; 
 ﻻزم ﺑﻪ ذﮐﺮ اﺳﺖ در اﻟﮕﻮرﯾﺘﻢdfs
 ، آراﯾـﻪVisited

Procedure bfs (V:Vertex); 
begin 
Visited[V]:=true; 
Addq(q, V); 
while not emptyqueu(q) do 
begin 
delq(q, V); 
for all nodes W adjacent to V do begin 
addq(q, W); 
Visited[W]:=true; 
end 
end 
end;

 دارد ﺑﻪ اﺳﻢ Collapsing Find(i) ﺑﻪ ﺷﮑﻞ زﯾﺮ
   :
for (int r=i; parent [r]> =0;  r=parent[r]);   //   find root 
while (i!=r)  { 
int S=Parent[i]; 
Parent[i]=r; 
i=S; 
} 
return r;

Xsort ( )
{ 
for i=2  to   n  do 
{ 
x=A [i] 
j=i-1; 
while (j>0 and A[j]>x) 
do 
{ 
A [j+1]=A [j]; 
j=j-1; 
} 
A [j+1]=x; 
} 
} // end Xsort
